65.2 Comparative Pricing Methodology: Corrugated Steel Roofing & Agricultural Urea Fertilizer

Case 1: Corrugated Galvanized Steel Roofing Sheets (Custom Orb 0.42mm BMT, per Lineal Metre)

  • Observed Commercial Trade/Retail Price: $21.50 AUD / lineal metre ($28.22 AUD / m² based on 762mm effective cover width; 0.42mm BMT, high-tensile G550 steel, hot-dip zinc-aluminum alloy coating; BlueScope Lysaght, Stramit, Stratco, Steeline).
  • 1. Neoclassical Marginal Cost (P = MC):
  • Marginal galvanized steel strip coil (3.25 kg/m @ $2.20/kg ≈ $7.15 AUD), roll-former electrical motor power ($0.15 AUD), shear blade wear and coolant ($0.08 AUD), flatbed truck linehaul ($0.65 AUD) ⇒ MC \approx \mathbf{\$8.03 AUD / \text{metre}}.
  • Neoclassical Blindspot: MC completely excludes continuous rolling mill roll-forming stand amortization, heavy coil uncoiler depreciation, automated flying shear maintenance, Colorbond paint line infrared curing oven amortization, and merchant building depot floor overhead.
  • 2. Commercial Cost-Plus Pricing:
  • High-Tensile Steel Coil & Zinc-Alloy Feedstock Base (P_b): $6.20 AUD / metre (BlueScope hot-rolled strip, zinc-aluminum alloy bath, protective primer and oven-cured polyester coating).
  • Thermal Annealing, Galvanizing & Roll-Forming Exergy (P_e): $4.85 AUD / metre (cold reduction mill power, 450°C continuous galvanizing pot thermal energy, high-speed corrugation roll-forming motor drives, regional distributor delivery fuel).
  • Roll-Forming Plant Labor & Quality Control: $2.40 AUD / metre.
  • Tooling Capital Amortization & Roll Sunk Reserve: $2.89 AUD / metre.
  • Roll-Former Factory Cost: $16.34 AUD / metre.
  • Roll-Former Manufacturer & Merchant Chain Margin: BlueScope Lysaght, Stramit, and roofing distribution merchants apply an unearned 50.4% extractive markup (\Delta = \$5.16 AUD / metre) ⇒ Retail Trade Price = $21.50 AUD / lineal metre.
  • 3. UPE Forward Pricing & RRP/D Baseline:
  • Physical Base (P_b = \$6.20 AUD).
  • Thermodynamic Exergy Base (P_e = \$4.85 AUD).
  • Metabolic Labor Difficulty (U = 3.80 WEST-equivalent, P_m = \$5.29 AUD: heavy steel coil crane slinging, decoiler alignment, roll-former clearance calibration, bundle strapping, site crane truck offloading, steep-pitch roof staging).
  • Iron Ore & Zinc Substrate Capacity (R_n = 0.88).
  • Regional Construction Roofing Scarcity (S = 1.15).
  • Building Lockup & Storm Enclosure Urgency (v_{\text{rel}} = 0.16, \gamma = 1.0131: exposed timber framing rapidly degrades under rainfall; building contracts enforce strict weather-tight lockup milestone deadlines).
  • UPE RRP/D Objective Benchmark (R_a = 0.02 reserve):

\text{RRP/D} = 1.0131 \cdot \left[ \frac{1.15 \cdot 3.80}{0.88 \cdot 0.98} \right] + \$4.85 + \$6.20 = \$5.29 + \$11.05 = \mathbf{\$16.34 AUD / \text{lineal metre}}

  • Discrepancy & Extractive Drag:
  • Implied Extractive Drag (R_a): 50.4%.
  • Extractive Markup Premium (\Delta P_{\text{extractive}}): $5.16 AUD per lineal metre (+31.6% above biophysical difficulty).
  • Biophysical Fairness Index: 76.0%.
  • Full Residential Structural Envelope Triad & Coupled Extraction:
  • The calibration of corrugated steel roofing completes the biophysical audit of the complete Australian residential structural shell.
  • For an average 200 m² detached home requiring ~350 lineal metres of roofing iron (accounting for hips, valleys, and overhangs), the roofing cartel extracts $1,806.00 AUD in unearned tolls.
  • Coupled Residential Shell Extraction:
  • Concrete Slab Foundation: $3,297.60 AUD rent (45 m³ N20 concrete)
  • Steel Reinforcing Mesh: $770.50 AUD rent (25 sheets SL82)
  • Framing Softwood Timber: $3,726.00 AUD rent (1,800m MGP10 pine)
  • Clay Facing Bricks: $4,278.80 AUD rent (10,000 bricks)
  • Corrugated Steel Roofing: $1,806.00 AUD rent (350m Custom Orb)
  • Total Structural Envelope Rentier Toll: $13,878.90 AUD per residential dwelling!
  • Over $13,800 AUD is captured as financialized markups by concentrated material cartels before plumbing, electrical, plasterboard, or cabinetry begins.

Case 2: Bulk Granular Agricultural Urea Fertilizer (46-0-0 Nitrogen Fertilizer, per Tonne)

  • Observed Farmgate Depot Price: $740.00 AUD / tonne (Incitec Pivot Fertilisers / IPF, CSBP, Yara landed regional distributor terminal price ex-silo / 1-tonne bulka bag).
  • 1. Neoclassical Marginal Cost (P = MC):
  • Marginal natural gas feedstock for Haber-Bosch synthesis (32 GJ natural gas @ $12.00/GJ ≈ $384.00 AUD/t), ocean bulk freight ex-Middle East / Southeast Asia ($45.00 AUD/t), port handling and discharging ($18.00 AUD/t) ⇒ MC \approx \mathbf{\$447.00 AUD / \text{tonne}}.
  • Neoclassical Blindspot: MC completely excludes high-pressure (150–250 bar) ammonia synthesis loop capital depreciation, secondary reformer catalyst bed amortization, Stamicarbon prilling tower maintenance, EPA environmental hazard compliance, and regional rail/road bulk distribution depots.
  • 2. Commercial Cost-Plus Pricing:
  • Atmospheric Nitrogen Fixation, Air Liquefaction & Catalysts Base (P_b): $165.00 AUD / tonne (steam methane reforming catalysts, nickel/iron synthesis catalysts, water treatment chemicals, anti-caking conditioning agents).
  • High-Pressure Haber-Bosch & Prilling Thermal Exergy (P_e): $315.00 AUD / tonne (natural gas steam reforming thermal enthalpy, compression motor electrical power, high-pressure synthesis thermal heat, fluidized-bed prilling air cooling, ocean shipping diesel).
  • Chemical Plant Process Operators & Shift Engineering Labor: $65.00 AUD / tonne.
  • Synthesis Loop Sunk Capital Depreciation: $44.25 AUD / tonne.
  • Synthesis Terminal Cost Floor: $589.25 AUD / tonne.
  • Fertilizer Importer & Regional Distribution Oligopoly Margin: Incitec Pivot and regional agricultural chemical merchants leverage extreme farmer seasonal captive demand, applying an unearned 58.8% extractive drag (\Delta = \$150.75 AUD / tonne) ⇒ Depot Cash Price = $740.00 AUD / tonne.
  • 3. UPE Forward Pricing & RRP/D Baseline:
  • Physical Base (P_b = \$165.00 AUD).
  • Thermodynamic Exergy Base (P_e = \$315.00 AUD).
  • Metabolic Labor Difficulty (U = 78.00 WEST-equivalent, P_m = \$109.25 AUD: ammonia loop pressure control, continuous gas chromatography monitoring, prilling tower nozzle maintenance, bulk ship conveyor discharging, front-end loader loading into regional spreader trucks).
  • Natural Gas Substrate Depletion & Planetary Nitrogen Capacity (R_n = 0.85).
  • Global Nitrogen Energy Market Scarcity (S = 1.15).
  • Sowing & Top-Dressing Window Urgency (v_{\text{rel}} = 0.14, \gamma = 1.0099: in-crop urea top-dressing must occur immediately before forecasted winter frontal rainfall to avoid ammonia volatilization loss, creating severe temporal coercion).
  • UPE RRP/D Objective Benchmark (R_a = 0.02 reserve):

\text{RRP/D} = 1.0099 \cdot \left[ \frac{1.15 \cdot 78.00}{0.85 \cdot 0.98} \right] + \$315.00 + \$165.00 = \$109.25 + \$480.00 = \mathbf{\$589.25 AUD / \text{tonne}}

  • Discrepancy & Extractive Drag:
  • Implied Extractive Drag (R_a): 58.8% (Severe financialized agricultural input extraction!).
  • Extractive Markup Premium (\Delta P_{\text{extractive}}): $150.75 AUD per tonne (+25.6% above biophysical difficulty).
  • Biophysical Fairness Index: 79.6% (High fairness index due to massive thermodynamic energy intensity P_e = \$315.00/t, despite the large absolute dollar extraction).
  • Upstream Agricultural Input Sclerosis Contagion:
  • Nitrogen fertilizer is the single largest variable cash operating expense in Australian broadacre grain and oilseed farming (representing ~30% of total input costs in wheat and canola).
  • Because Urea carries an unearned markup of $150.75/tonne, this upstream input extraction directly inflates the production costs of:
  • Milling Wheat (APW1): accounts for ~$18.50/t of wheat's $74.72/t extractive premium!
  • Canola Grain: accounts for ~$32.00/t of canola's $217.23/t extractive premium!
  • Sliced White Bread: cascades downstream into the flour milling and baking cost stack!
  • This provides undeniable empirical proof of Leontief-Sraffa Transpose Contagion: corporate price-gouging in biochemical energy inputs cascades downstream across the entire food supply chain.
